IN-LEVEL overlay note - cs+0x000 to +0x180 is sentinel-init (0x7FFF0000 pattern), the HeroCamMotion sub-block cs+0x1A0 (0x1B5320, written by TrackHeroMotionForCamera) is live at static offsets in-level, but cs+0x190 activeCamera reads UNMAPPED 0x089C1000 in-level (overlay-resident camera object, NOT a frontend pointer) - so the camera transform heads (camPos +0x140, matrix) are overlay-managed not at these frontend offsets. See tools/native/camera_map_re.md

// === Track-B 989snd EE->IOP command-id wrapper map (cod/0321A0 unit, block 0x132828..0x133988). ===
// DOC-ONLY (no active name=addr): every entry below is a thin per-command wrapper already present
// as matched bare-C or INCLUDE_ASM in src/usa/cod/0321A0.c, so adding active symbols here would
// desync the carve. Recorded for the strategic M3-audio read. CLASSIFICATION: snd_QueueCommandToRing
// entries are ASYNC (ring-buffered, NON-blocking, CPU-SIDE marshalling - portable). snd_SendCommandSync
// entries are SYNC (BLOCKING sceSifCallRpc to the IOP - IOP/SPU-HARDWARE-BOUNDARY, M3-audio territory,

// === Track-B localization / text-table + subtitle system (EU anchors - string content + table layout vary per language). ===

//   +0 char* str | +4 int textId | +8 int voiceClip (-1 none) | +0xC reserved (0x10 total).

g_discToc = 0x0014B540; // CONFIRMED - master disc asset directory - 0x5800 bytes from LBA 0x3e9 via LoadDiscToc - holds WAD base LBAs at +0x32c/+0x36c, global-asset sector pair tables 0x14c310/0x14c338/0x14f368 stride 8, text-table offset, per-level TOC directory at 0x150538

// === Track-B 3D positional sound-emitter system (the FUN_002E6B30/2E6BD8 helpers - REFUTED as particle FX, they are sound emitters). ===
StartSoundEmitter = 0x002E6908; // CONFIRMED - core 3D sound start - validates sound def, allocs slot via AllocVoiceHandleSlot, fills g_soundEmitterTable entry (def +0x8, volScale +0x10, owner moby +0x18, pos +0x20), culls if ComputeEmitterVolume below 0x20, random pitch from def +0x10..+0x14, sets state 7 pending key-on, returns slot idx or -1
PlayMobySound = 0x002E6B30; // CONFIRMED - plays sound #idx from the owner moby class sound bank (moby +0x24 class header - +0xD count byte, +0x28 def array, 0x20 stride) via StartSoundEmitter, records soundIdx and owner in slot - callers incl vendor menus and wrench - prior FX-spawn breadcrumb corrected, this is audio
PlaySoundFromClassBank = 0x002E6BD8; // CONFIRMED - same as PlayMobySound but resolves the class sound bank by moby class id via header table 0x1CDB00 indexed by remap byte table 0x1CE460 - UpdateNanotechOrb ids 0x200/0x1F5 are pickup sounds - Ghidra validator forced alias PlaySoundFromClassBank
PlayGlobalSound = 0x002E6C90; // CONFIRMED - plays from the global/common sound-def pool g_pGlobalSoundDefs bounded by g_nGlobalSoundDefs - the UI/system sound entry used by ~60 menu/UI callers (MenuScreenDoAction, UpdateSkillPointsMenu, vendor, map, subtitles, popups)
UpdateSoundEmitters = 0x002E5900; // CONFIRMED - per-frame driver - listener pos history + velocity, master volume ducking, per-slot owner-follow, distance volume, occlusion ring sampling, then issues 989snd commands (deferred key-on FUN_00132a10 with g_soundBankHandles, snd_SetVoiceVolumeRamp with ComputeEmitterPan, key-off) plus UpdateDialogVoiceManager - the proof this pool is audio not particles
ComputeEmitterVolume = 0x002E54D8; // CONFIRMED - distance from listener (camera pos 0x1B52C0) to emitter then ComputeVolumeFalloff vs the slot sound def - result below 0x20 means inaudible cull
ComputeVolumeFalloff = 0x002E53E0; // CONFIRMED - interpolates def far volume (+0x8) to near volume (+0xC) across minDist..maxDist - def +0x19 bit0 selects squared vs linear falloff
ComputeEmitterPan = 0x002E5520; // CONFIRMED - emitter direction in camera space (matrix 0x1B54F0) - atan2 azimuth with hysteresis vs slot +0x40 last angle for near sources - returns degrees, used as pan arg of snd_SetVoiceVolumeRamp
CastEmitterOcclusionRay = 0x002E5340; // CONFIRMED - raycast (mask 0x82, ignore owner moby) toward a point 75 percent from listener to emitter clamped to 64.0 - result feeds the 36-entry per-slot occlusion history ring at +0x44, occluded fraction halves volume
ComputeListenerOcclusionProbe = 0x002E5290; // CONFIRMED - random jittered probe point (0.5..6.0) around the listener (g_flCameraPos 0x1B52C0), raycast via CollLine mask 0x82, on hit the probe is pulled to 75% toward g_collHitPoint (0x1BAF20, the ray's hit surface) - fills g_listenerOcclusionProbes - 6 regenerated on camera-id change, used to occlusion-test pending (state 7) sounds before key-on
InitSoundEmitterSystem = 0x002E5720; // CONFIRMED - boot init from FUN_002914d8 - clears listener ring + all slot states, snd_Init(2), mono/stereo from 0x1A7BA0, channel limits, applies g_sndChannelVolumes, default reverb setup
StopSoundEmitter = 0x002E6830; // CONFIRMED - state 7 pending slots freed immediately, live slots set to state 4 stop-requested which UpdateSoundEmitters keys off to state 6
StopAllSoundEmitters = 0x002E6E18; // CONFIRMED - flush 989snd ring, pump until idle, kill all voices, clear listener ring and every slot state - called at level teardown FUN_002ed260 and PlayBonusMovie
IsMobySoundActive = 0x002E67E8; // CONFIRMED - returns 1 if slot owner +0x18 equals the moby and state is 1 or 2 (keyed-on/playing)
SetSoundEmitterOffset = 0x002E6D38; // CONFIRMED - switches a slot to offset-follow mode - sets SE_FLAG_OFFSET_MODE (0x40) in flags +0x5 and stores a vec4 into localOffset +0x30 (0x188700 block), UpdateSoundEmitters then transforms it by the owner moby matrix +0xC0 each frame - callers FUN_00273D20, FUN_00273EA8
OnEmitterVoiceKeyedOn = 0x002E6ED8; // CONFIRMED - 989snd callback for deferred key-on - stores voice handle at slot +0x0 and advances state 1 to 2, zero handle frees the slot - function created this run (was undefined bytes)
OnEmitterVoiceEnded = 0x002E6F20; // CONFIRMED - 989snd callback - frees the emitter slot (state 0, owner and link cleared) when called with handle 0 - function created this run (was undefined bytes)
LoadGlobalSoundBank = 0x002B76A8; // CONFIRMED - async load of the boot/global sample bank via snd_BankLoadAsync into g_soundBankHandles[0] - writes -1 loading sentinel first
LoadLevelSoundBank = 0x002B7700; // CONFIRMED - loads a sample bank from EE memory via snd_BankLoadFromEE_CB into g_soundBankHandles[idx] - called from LoadLevelAndInitHealth with the level segment bank
OnSoundBankLoaded = 0x002B7690; // CONFIRMED - bank-load completion callback - stores the returned bank handle into the g_soundBankHandles entry passed as context - function created this run
g_soundEmitterTable = 0x001886D0; // CONFIRMED - 3D sound emitter slot pool - 52 entries x 0x70 - entry +0x0 voice handle, +0x4 state byte, +0x5 flags, +0x8 def ptr, +0x10 volume scale, +0x12 occlusion cursor, +0x14 pitch, +0x18 owner moby, +0x20 pos vec4, +0x40 last pan angle, +0x44 occlusion ring u8[36] - NOTE existing g_voiceHandleTable 0x1886D4 is the +0x4 state field of entry 0, real base is here
g_listenerPosHistory = 0x00188660; // CONFIRMED - ring of 4 vec4 listener positions pushed per frame by UpdateSoundEmitters - deltas averaged into listener velocity
g_listenerPosHistoryIdx = 0x001886A0; // CONFIRMED - write index 0..3 into g_listenerPosHistory advanced mod 4 per frame
g_globalSoundDefsPtr = 0x001B162C; // CONFIRMED - pointer to the global/common sound-def pool - set to &g_globalSoundDefs (0x238C00) in LoadLevelAndInitHealth, read by PlayGlobalSound
g_nGlobalSoundDefs = 0x001A8BBC; // CONFIRMED - count of global sound defs - set to 0x14 in LoadLevelAndInitHealth, bound check in PlayGlobalSound
g_globalSoundDefs = 0x00238C00; // CONFIRMED - static array of 0x14 sound defs (0x20-byte each - +0x8 far vol, +0xC near vol, +0x10/+0x14 pitch range, +0x18 enable gate, +0x1A sample id, +0x1C bank index) for UI/menu/system sounds
g_soundBankHandles = 0x00189E00; // CONFIRMED - s32 array of loaded 989snd bank handles indexed by def +0x1C bank index - -1 while loading, filled by OnSoundBankLoaded, cleared at level teardown - gates voice key-on in UpdateSoundEmitters
g_listenerOcclusionProbes = 0x00189DA0; // CONFIRMED - 6 x vec4 jittered probe points around the listener (each 0.5..6.0 random offset, biased toward g_collHitPoint on ray hit - see ComputeListenerOcclusionProbe), regenerated on camera change (id 0x1B1608, cache 0x189D98) - pending (state 7) sounds occlusion-test all 6 before key-on
g_sndChannelVolumes = 0x001886A8; // CONFIRMED mechanism - 7-entry per-channel master volume mix block computed by ComputeAudioChannelMix (0x2E5698) from the two audio-options sliders, applied via FUN_00132888(ch, vol). ch0 +0 = music*7/10, ch1 +4 = g_sfxVolume (the only channel fed by the SFX slider), ch2 +8 = music*1, ch3 +0xC = music*6/10, ch4 +0x10 = fixed 0x266, ch5 +0x14 = music*0x19>>5. ComputeAudioChannelMix writes ch0..ch5 (+0..+0x14), ch6 has no own slot - InitSoundEmitterSystem feeds it by re-reading the ch2 (music) source. So most channels track g_musicVolume, ch1 is the lone SFX-slider channel. Pause/menu ducking applied on top. Which physical channel carries the streamed music track still not pinned
// --- Track-B audio options (title + in-game audio menus; persisted with game settings) ---
g_musicVolume = 0x001A7BA8; // CONFIRMED mechanism, label STRONG - audio-options row-0 volume slider (0..0x400). Adjusted by UpdateAudioOptionsMenu 0x2D87C8 (title) and UpdateAudioOptionsMenuInGame 0x33E070 (in-game), both call ComputeAudioChannelMix on change. Scales ch0/ch2/ch3/ch5/ch6 of g_sndChannelVolumes. Music(row0)/Effects(row1) per RC2 audio-menu order, on-disc localized label string id 0x2DA5 not statically verified. Ghidra alias g_nMusicVolume
g_sfxVolume = 0x001A7BA4; // CONFIRMED mechanism, label STRONG - audio-options row-1 volume slider (0..0x400). Same two menu handlers, feeds only ch1 of g_sndChannelVolumes. Effects/SFX per RC2 menu order (label id 0x2DA6 on-disc, not statically verified). Ghidra alias g_nSfxVolume
g_audioStereoMode = 0x001A7BA0; // CONFIRMED - audio-options row-2 toggle (0 mono / nonzero stereo). Toggled on X in both audio menus, then FUN_00132938(stereo==0) pushes mono/stereo to the snd driver, also read at InitSoundEmitterSystem boot. Menu shows label 0x2DA7 with value 0x2DA8 (mono) / 0x2DA9 (stereo). Ghidra alias g_nAudioStereoMode

// === Track-B camera system (per-frame camera update, slot arbitration + camvtbl mode dispatch). ===
UpdateCamera = 0x00272378; // CONFIRMED - master per-frame camera update - fades, hero tracking, mode dispatch, transition blend or snap from active slot, euler + shake + underwater + fog + FOV steps - in-ELF callers EnterVendorMenu and exit cinematic, gameplay call comes from overlays
DispatchCameraMode = 0x00270A58; // CONFIRMED - scans 48 camera slots for takeover, switches active camera, then calls mode update handler at camvtbl + modeId*0x14 + 0xC (modeId short at cam+0x8C) - saves pos +0x30 to prevPos +0x64
TestCameraTakeover = 0x00270848; // CONFIRMED - returns 1 if candidate slot should take over - defers to camvtbl+4 check fn (-1 forbid, 1 force) then rule at +0x74 - 0 priority byte +0x7C compare, 1/2 trigger flag +0x7D, 4 hero-pos sphere test vs config table 0x1B1484, 7 type match vs 0x18C2C0
SwitchActiveCamera = 0x002705A8; // CONFIRMED - activates new camera slot - picks transition kind/duration (default 0.018), saves old cam to g_prevCamera with history copy 0x1B76B0 to 0x1B7930, resets g_cameraFramesSinceCut, calls enter handler, snaps g_cameraPos to new cam pos +0x30
CallCameraEnterHandler = 0x00270560; // CONFIRMED - invokes camvtbl entry +0x8 (enter) for the camera mode if non-null - handler bodies live in level overlays
CallCameraPollHandler = 0x00270A10; // PARTIAL - invokes camvtbl entry +0x10 for the current camera at top of dispatch each frame - mechanism traced, exact role of this vtbl slot unconfirmed
BeginCameraTransition = 0x00270EF0; // CONFIRMED - arms the camera transition interpolator - kind 0 captures source pos 0x1B5460 + quat 0x1B5470, kind 2 captures 0x1B54D0/E0, else hero-relative blend matrix 0x1B5480 - sets state 3
ApplyCameraTransition = 0x002718C8; // CONFIRMED - per-frame transition blend toward target camera matrix via 0x271140 (quat slerp) or 0x2712E8 (matrix blend) - on completion copies final matrix + pos and clears transition state
ApplyCameraShakeAxis = 0x00271968; // CONFIRMED - applies one shake channel to the camera - axis 0/1 offset g_cameraPos along matrix up/right rows, axis 2 rolls g_cameraMatrix - sin-windowed decay - channels at 0x1B52E0 0x1B52F0 0x1B5300 - skipped for camera type 6
CheckCameraUnderwater = 0x00271E98; // CONFIRMED - sets g_bCameraUnderwater via up to 6 vertical collision rays (mask 0x12) from camera pos and water height query 0x2A9F60 - render loop draws water tint from it
TrackHeroMotionForCamera = 0x00271BA0; // CONFIRMED mechanics (native #else done, matching build INCLUDE_ASM) - smooths hero facing 0x1B5340 (spring func_002702D8 on negated 0x18A0E0), velocity 0x1B5390, speed 0x1B53C0, forward/lateral split 0x1B53A0/B0/C4/C8, spring-smoothed height 0x1B5328, prev pos 0x1B5380, 5-entry orient-Z ring 0x1B53CC, all in the g_cameraState+0x1A0 sub-block. Consumers presumed overlay camera handlers, not yet traced
UpdateScreenFadeBlack = 0x002721D8; // CONFIRMED - drives g_screenFadeBlack toward target 0x1B53EC at rate 0x1B53F0 while request flag 0x1B53E8 set
UpdateScreenFadeWhite = 0x00272290; // CONFIRMED - drives g_screenFadeWhite toward target 0x1B53FC at rate 0x1B5400 while request flag 0x1B53F4 set - white-flash twin of the black fade
StepCameraFovInterp = 0x00270028; // CONFIRMED - camera FOV interpolation - advances g_cameraFov between base 0x1B5554 and target 0x1B5570 per mode 0x1B5568 (snap / sin-eased / angle-lerp / spring) then writes g_cameraProjScale = cot(fov/2) and rebuilds projection
BuildCameraProjection = 0x0027B0A0; // CONFIRMED - rebuilds full projection/viewport parameter set from g_cameraProjScale - vertical scale 0.775 NTSC / 0.756 PAL (flag 0x1A7B98) and 0.75 widescreen (byte 0x1A7BB9) - GS matrices 0x1B9080+ and render context mirrors 0x1B9280+ 0x2633A0+
SampleCameraFogZone = 0x002735B0; // PARTIAL - resolves which environment zone contains the camera (0x2A7490, zone records 0x1C1710 stride 0x80) and blends two RGB colors + near/far params into 0x1B15C0-0x1B15D0 - believed fog color/planes, consumer not yet traced
MatrixToEulerAngles = 0x002AC540; // CONFIRMED - converts 3x4 rotation matrix to euler angles via successive atan2 + axis rotations - used by UpdateCamera to fill g_cameraRot from g_cameraMatrix
ApplyCinematicCameraKey = 0x002E78B8; // PARTIAL - applies one cinematic camera keyframe from list 0x1B8828 (stride 0x20 - pos, euler rotZ/Y/X, proj scale +0x1C) to camera pos/matrix/projection - used by level-exit ship cinematic path (caller 0x2E91C0, game state 6)
g_cameraPos = 0x001B52C0; // CONFIRMED - camera world position vec4 - written by switch/transition/cinematic paths - read engine-wide for distance and culling - debug overlay prints it as Cam - Ghidra alias g_flCameraPos float[4]

g_activeCamera = 0x001B5310; // CONFIRMED - pointer to active 0xA0-byte camera slot - fields +0x00 matrix +0x30 pos +0x74 rule +0x7C priority +0x84 config index +0x86 type +0x8C mode id +0x8E snap flag - Ghidra alias g_pActiveCamera

// === Track-B level staging / loading-scene / scene loader / menu-bg streamer (the CD-streamer consumers). ===
UpdateLevelStagingMachine = 0x00294310; // CONFIRMED - per-tick staging state machine 0-5 - states 0/1 KickRawFileRead the two level chunks (level TOC pairs 0x1507e8/0x1507f8 + base 0x1507dc) below the 0x1F0C000 ceiling - 2 waits g_bLoadingSceneBanksHeld then StartLevelMusicStream + LoadGlobalSoundBank - 3 waits g_soundBankHandles[0] - 4 KickLevelBankDiscLoad(0) - 5 waits handle then done returns 1 - stall over 0x2d0 ticks or RPC error drops spindlctrl 0x1a7900 to 0 and rewinds one state to retry
StartFrontendSegmentLoad = 0x00294280; // CONFIRMED - quit-to-menu path - carves buffer below 0x1FF7FF0 from frontend TOC pair 0x14b888/0x14b88c - stores in g_pLoadedSegment with 0x60 header slack word - KickRawFileRead at lbn ofs+g_levelWadBaseLbn

// === Track-B moby / game-object entity system (engine side - per-moby update funcs are overlay-resident). ===
// Moby entity record (0x100 bytes) layout is recovered in include/moby.h (24 offsets static-asserted
// against the SCUS_972.68 readers/writers). Key fields: +0x00 bsphere, +0x10 position, +0x20 state,
// +0x22 classSlot, +0x34 modeFlags, +0x64 pUpdate(overlay), +0x68 aux/vars, +0xAA classId, +0xC0 matrix,
// +0xF0 facing/heading. SpawnMoby/FreeMoby/UpdateActiveMobys are the pool spawn/free/per-frame walk.
SpawnMoby = 0x0029FE08; // CONFIRMED (fail printf "Tried to create moby and couldn't" 0x1A9DC8) - scans g_mobySpawnStart..g_mobyTableEnd stride 0x100 for a slot with state +0x20 >= 0xFE whose release time +0xA0 <= g_gameTime, calls InitMobyFromClass, attaches the 0x80-byte aux block (moby+0x68 = g_mobyAuxBlockBase + index*0x80, memset 0), decrements g_mobySpawnCredit, returns Moby* or 0
InitMobyFromClass = 0x0029FF00; // CONFIRMED - memset(moby,0,0x100) then stamps defaults +0x22 classSlot=g_mobyClassSlotRemap[classId], +0x23 alpha 0x80, +0xAA classId, +0xAC uid (slotIndex<<16), +0x38 color qword, +0x2C scale, +0x48/+0x4C anim rates 1.0, if class loaded binds +0x64 pUpdate from g_mobyClassUpdateFuncs[slot], +0x24 pClass header, +0x78 collMesh, mode flags - else headerless table + flags|=5/7

UpdateActiveMobys = 0x002B7578; // CONFIRMED - per-frame walk - BuildActiveMobyChain into g_activeMobyChainHead then for each chained moby runs UpdateMobyAnimation unless mode 0x40, calls pUpdate +0x64 with moby arg, then UpdateMobyBSphereAndGrid unless mode 4 - the per-class pUpdate bodies live in level overlays not this ELF
BuildActiveMobyChain = 0x002A16D8; // CONFIRMED - walks the moby table - distance-culls pos +0x10 vs g_flCameraPos against drawDist byte +0x30 squared, buckets survivors by class slot +0x22 into scratchpad lists linked via +0x28, whole-group pull-in via group byte +0x21 and g_mobyGroupLists, collects mode-0x1000 mobys to g_mobyFlagged1000List, returns chain head
UpdateMobyAnimation = 0x002A13E0; // CONFIRMED - advances anim time +0x44 by rate +0x48 stepping frames +0x40/+0x41 and seqs +0x42/+0x43 from class hdr+0x48 seq table, updates frame ptrs +0x58/+0x5C and flags +0x60, fires anim-keyed sound events via PlayMobySound from the seq event array
ResolveMobyAnimFramePtrs = 0x002A01C8; // CONFIRMED - refreshes +0x58/+0x5C from seq frame ptr arrays (seq +0x1C) plus +0x6C loop sound idx from seq+0x11 - seq 0xFF means procedural frames at g_proceduralAnimFrames stride 0x800
UpdateMobyAnimLoopSound = 0x002A0288; // CONFIRMED - maintains the looping seq sound - +0x6C sound idx, +0x6D emitter slot - starts PlayMobySound mode 4, stops emitter when seq changed or slot stolen or mode 0x40 or +0x7C bit 0x8000
UpdateMobyBSphereAndGrid = 0x002A1D80; // CONFIRMED - computes bounding sphere at +0x00 from pos +0x10 and anim-frame bounds via VU0 microprogram 0xD18 (or matrix rows +0xC0 when mode 0x100), bumps dirty counter +0xAC, re-buckets in the spatial grid via UpdateMobyGridCells when the quantized cell range vs cache +0xA4 changed - bsphere semantic from scale/pos math, grid mechanics fully traced
UpdateMobyGridCells = 0x002A1AA0; // CONFIRMED - spatial hash maintenance - removes moby index from cells in cached range +0xA4..+0xA7 and re-inserts at the new packed range - cells g_mobyGridCells (u16 block + u8 count + u8 cap), u16-index blocks g_mobyGridBlocks, bitmap allocator g_mobyGridBlockBitmap
AllocMobyGridBlockBits = 0x002A1A20; // CONFIRMED - first-fit allocation of n contiguous bits in g_mobyGridBlockBitmap returning block index
ReleaseMobyGridBlockBits = 0x002A19C0; // CONFIRMED - clears n bits from a block index in g_mobyGridBlockBitmap - traps on double free
RegisterMobyClass = 0x00294140; // CONFIRMED - assigns a class slot and fills g_mobyClassSlotRemap / g_mobyClassSlotToId / g_mobyClassHeaders / g_mobyClassDataSizes (hdr byte +0x2D << 10), binds update fn, then FixupMobyClassHeader - headerless variant only takes a slot from g_mobyClassCountNoHeader
FixupMobyClassHeader = 0x00293DA8; // CONFIRMED - rebases loaded class header offsets to pointers - mesh blocks, sound defs +0x28, anim seq array +0x48 (count byte +0xC, frame ptrs seq+0x1C) - and copies the 16-byte bounds vec into g_mobyClassBounds[slot]
BindMobyClassUpdateFunc = 0x002B74D8; // CONFIRMED - resolves classId in builtin binding table g_builtinMobyUpdateBindings (0xC-byte entries, -1 terminated) into g_mobyClassUpdateFuncs or the headerless table - table is empty in the shipped ELF so real bindings are installed by overlay code (writer UNCONFIRMED)
LoadMobyClassFromWad = 0x00294F70; // PARTIAL - on-demand single class load - DecompressWad to 0x178510 via TOC 0x150084 stride 0x14, optional texture upload, RegisterMobyClass into a temporary top slot then restore count - which models (armor/gadget/menu) not traced
SwapMobyTableContext = 0x0028BD78; // CONFIRMED - toggles g_activeMobyTableId and exchanges base/spawnStart/end/aux globals with the HUD shadow set 0x1B182C/30/60/64 - HUD elements are mobys in a 40-slot side table
InitHudMobyTable = 0x0028B5B0; // CONFIRMED - hud.cpp init (debug __FILE__ 0x1A8E08) - clears the 13 HUD element slots and DebugMallocs the HUD moby table 0x2800 = 40 x 0x100 plus 0x1400 aux, marks slot 0 free, calls ResetBoltCounterHud
ReloadAllHudBankTextures = 0x0028B6F0; // CONFIRMED - hud.cpp - re-streams the 4 HUD texture banks (idx 0/2/3/4, header flags +0x15/+0x17/+0x18/+0x19) after VRAM reorg - TransferIopRingData using the stored ring handle (header +0x25/+0x27/+0x28/+0x29), DecompressWad, then RelocateHudBankGsSlots - mode arg gates banks 3/4 - called from MenuScreenLoad
RelocateHudBankGsSlots = 0x0028B8C8; // CONFIRMED - hud.cpp asset fixup - for HUD bank's CLUT (g_pHudClutSlots) + texture (g_pHudTextureSlots) GS-slot words adds the new VRAM base and clears the 0x80000000 needs-relocation flag, guarded by header+0x1d so it relocates once - inverse of InvalidateHudBankGsSlots
InvalidateHudBankGsSlots = 0x0028BA28; // CONFIRMED - hud.cpp - inverse of RelocateHudBankGsSlots - subtracts the applied VRAM base back out of a HUD bank's CLUT+texture GS slots and re-sets the 0x80000000 flag, clears the bank reloc marker +0x74 - called from FUN_002cb560 post-VRAM-rebuild menu transition
UploadHudBankTextures = 0x0028BBA0; // CONFIRMED - hud.cpp - iterates a HUD bank's texture slots and uploads each to GS via UploadTextureToGs, advancing a VRAM cursor (DAT_001a7308) by 2^(logW+logH)*4, writes assigned GS page into slot high half, relocates the bank first if needed - called from ParseLoadedSegment + FUN_002cb560
UploadTextureToGs = 0x002901B0; // CONFIRMED - builds a GS image-upload (TRXPOS/TRXREG/TRXDIR) GIF packet for one texture via BuildGsImageUploadPacket - kickNow==0 splices a DMA tag chain into g_pFrameDmaCursor for deferred upload, else FlushCache + KickGifImageUpload issues it now - generic single-texture GS uploader (only caller is UploadHudBankTextures)
DecompressHudBankWad = 0x002933D0; // CONFIRMED - loaders.cpp/hud.cpp - if byteLen rounded to 0x10 nonzero DecompressWad's the HUD bank compressed sub-section (g_pLoadedSegment[bankIdx*2+8]) into place, then clears the bank reloc marker header+0x1d - called from ParseLoadedSegment
RenderMobys = 0x002A11F8; // CONFIRMED - per-frame render driver - sets up VU/DMA chain buffers then g_mobyVuChainCursor = BuildMobyVuChain(g_mobyTableBase, cursor, -1, 1) - prints the overflow string mobys dropped at 0x1A9E48 when chain budget exceeded

// --- moby anim-sequence setters + procedural slot drivers (GHIDRA-ONLY: all INCLUDE_ASM in split units text/1A00F0 + text/1A8180; named in Ghidra, NOT emitted active to avoid glabel desync) ---
// SetMobyAnimSequence            0x002A8200 - CONFIRMED instant seq set: animSeq +0x42/+0x43=seqIdx, clamp startFrame to seq frameCount (+0x10), set animFrame +0x40/+0x41 wrap, ResolveMobyAnimFramePtrs, cache +0x4C=*framePtr, clear advance bit2 +0x60, loopSound +0x6C=seq+0x11
// SetMobyAnimSequenceBlended     0x002A82D8 - CONFIRMED seq set with blendFrames: blendFrames<1 -> SetMobyAnimSequence; else (if anim mid-play/+0x50/+0x54 set) AcquireProceduralAnimSlot + BakeMobyProceduralFrame(slot|0x300) snapshot current pose into procedural frame, stash old seq +0xA9, set seq 0xFF, animRate +0x48=1.0, animTime=0, +0x4C=1/blendFrames
// SetMobyAnimSequenceBlendedEx   0x002A8448 - CONFIRMED as Blended but takes explicit startFrame (param_3) + flags (param_5): flags&4 force-bake, flags&1->slot|0x100, flags&2->slot|0x200 passed to BakeMobyProceduralFrame
// AcquireProceduralAnimSlot      0x002A08C0 - CONFIRMED first-fit/reuse moby's index in g_proceduralAnimSlotOwners[0..0xF], resets g_proceduralAnimSlotTimer entry, returns slot or -1 if full
// UpdateProceduralAnimSlots      0x002A0958 - CONFIRMED per-frame driver over the 0x10 slots: releases owners whose moby is gone or mode&0x80, for procedural mobys (seq 0xFF) increments timer and at >=0x14 re-fires UpdateMobyAnimation (anim time reset 1.0-+0x4C) when seq hold byte seq+0x13==0xFF
// ResetProceduralAnimSlots       0x002A0918 - CONFIRMED zeroes both g_proceduralAnimSlotOwners and g_proceduralAnimSlotTimer (16 entries each)
// BakeMobyProceduralFrame        0x002A3288 - CONFIRMED VU0+scratchpad-DMA pose baker: reads frame ptrs +0x58/+0x5C and class skeleton block (hdr+0x18 data, hdr+0x8 bone count), decompresses quantized rot/trans keyframes, interpolates A->B by blend +0x44, normalizes quaternions (_vrsqrt), DMAs result to g_proceduralAnimFrames + (slot&0xff)*0x800; arg flag bits 0x100/0x200 gate the two write passes
g_pHeroMoby = 0x0018C0B0; // CONFIRMED identity - hero (Ratchet) moby pointer. ClassifyTargetProximity/AcquireMobyAutoTarget load it as the lock-on reference, measure distance from candidates to (g_pHeroMoby+0x10)=its world position, return it as the auto-target anchor, CalcMobyTargetThreatDist biases +42 toward it. moby+0x10 pos == g_heroPos. Zeroed by InitLoadingSceneSystem, set to the class-0 cast member by cinematic playback FUN_002ec038. Live per-frame gameplay writer is overlay-side (0x01800000), so the gameplay assignment is not in this image, but every reader treats it as the player. Read by 90+ systems
g_heroState = 0x0018C0B4; // CONFIRMED - hero (Ratchet) movement/action state code (small int), right after g_pHeroMoby. Branched on by many readers SpawnThrusterExhaustFlameFx denser/brighter exhaust on states 8 and 0x7b(123) (boost), UpdateLockOnScannerGadget extends scan range on 0x1d, TrackHeroMotionForCamera special-cases !=0x11, also grab/throw + turret + bolt-pickup + nanotech FX. Exact enum value->action map (idle/walk/run/jump/grind/swim/boost) UNCONFIRMED, per-frame writer overlay-side
g_heroFacingDir = 0x0018A0E0; // CONFIRMED - hero (Ratchet) facing/forward unit direction vec3 (0x18A0E0..E8). Reset by InitLoadingSceneSystem to (0,0,-1) default -Z, same block that zeroes g_pHeroMoby. TrackHeroMotionForCamera negates it (len -1.0) to derive smoothed camera-facing 0x1B5340. Direction PARAM for billboard/decal sprite draws + gadget-beam arc builders. Per-frame writer overlay-side
g_cameraZoneType = 0x0018C2C0; // PARTIAL - current camera zone/type id (int). Only 2 readers in this image, both reads (writer overlay-side). TestCameraTakeover rule-7 slots take over only when their type field (+0x86) equals it - TrackHeroMotionForCamera bypasses the hero-height spring smoothing (snaps x/y only) when it equals 0x50. Exact value->zone map UNCONFIRMED. EU TODO

BootSystemInit = 0x002914D8; // CONFIRMED - one-shot boot bring-up at top of LoadLevelAndInitHealth - hw resets, SetVideoMode, vblank ISR, IOP reboot with IOPRP255.IMG, RPC/cd re-init, volume-ID sector to BuildSaveGamePaths, LoadDiscToc, boot WAD decompress to 0x614000 + 10 IRX loads, controller/mc/sound/file-load init, RCNT1 start, cinematic queue reset - full order in plate comment

DebugPrintStub = 0x0026FEC8; // CONFIRMED - DebugPrintStub(const char*fmt,...) retail NO-OP. Body only spills varargs a1-t3 + FP f12-f18 to own frame, no output/writes/callees. PURE shim = empty body. Receives debug strings (0x1A9060 Rebooted IOP, 0x1A9070 loaded irx modules)
InitControllers = 0x002B9468; // CONFIRMED - sceDbcInit then opens controller ports 0 and 1 - handles at 0x138314/0x138694 - g_padButtonsHeld/Pressed live inside the port-0 buffer block
UpdateController = 0x002B9518; // CONFIRMED (Track-B) - polls ONE controller-state struct (arg = 0x138180 or 0x138500 base, +0x194 holds scePad port handle) - reads scePadGetState (func_00128c18) into +0x19c then drives the connect state-machine at +0x198 - state 1 scePadRead (func_00128a50) into stack buf then ParsePadInput plus scePadSetActAlign (func_00128fd0) for rumble - state 0 queries pad info modes (func_00128b28 plus func_00128f50) into +0x180/+0x184 and resets via ResetControllerState - any disconnect or unsupported pad calls ResetControllerState - called twice per frame by func_002B9F20 (the pad-injection hook target) for both ports - NOTE the scePad* names are SCE libpad identifications inferred from RPC selector and packet shape, the func_ addresses are authoritative
ResetControllerState = 0x002B97A8; // CONFIRMED (Track-B) - clears ONE controller's parsed input block - zeroes the 16-word analog/button history at +0x140 and the decoded-state fields +0x1a0..+0x1d8 - sets +0x1d0/+0x1d4 to 1 - called by UpdateController on disconnect or pad-info refresh
ParsePadInput = 0x002B9810; // CONFIRMED (Track-B) - decodes a raw scePadRead packet (param_2) into the controller struct - button word at +0x1a0/+0x1b0 is the active-low pad bitmask inverted (xor 0xffff) - 4 analog axes at +0x100 normalized from 0x7f center - per-button pressure at +0x110 scaled by 1/255 - builds pressed/released/held edge masks at +0x1a4/+0x1a8/+0x1b4/+0x1c4/+0x1c8 from prior state +0x1ac/+0x1bc - maintains a 0x1e-entry analog-stick magnitude/angle ring (Vec2LengthVu0 plus Atan2fPoly) at +0x190 for flick detection (sets bit 0x10000 on a fast stick sweep) - and an auto-repeat counter at +0x34e/+0x34f - reads DAT_001a7a4c (invert option flag, PROBABLE) which negates axes 0x100/0x108/0x140/0x148
SetVideoMode = 0x002849B0; // CONFIRMED - ResetVif1AndGif then sceGsResetGraph - g_bProgressiveScan selects (0,0,0x50,1) DTV480p else interlaced mode 3 PAL when g_bPalMode set, 2 NTSC otherwise
SetupGsDisplayBuffers = 0x0027BAA0; // CONFIRMED - CONFIRMED - sets up GS draw/display buffer layout by video mode - PAL branch uses draw 0x100000 / disp 0x1E0000 (0x200x0x1C0 dims, flag 4), progressive branch keeps 0x118000/0x1E8000 with the 0x280-wide (0x200,0x1a0,0x280,0x1c0, flag 0) setup, NTSC default otherwise
InitScreenGeometry = 0x0027AF50; // PARTIAL - recomputes screen half-extents + scissor from display dims 0x1A65D0/D2 and seeds projection constants incl g_flCameraProjScale 0.62 - boundary with the camera system not fully traced

AppendDmaCntTagWithVifCode = 0x002FD3A8; // CONFIRMED - appends one qword [0x10000000, 0, 0, vifcode] to the frame DMA chain at cursor 0x1B2228 (advances 0x10) - DMAtag ID=CNT QWC=0 carrying NOP + the caller vifcode in its upper qword - sole caller FUN_00285ef8 passes 0x13000000 = VIF1 FLUSHA to bracket a GIF packet

InstallVif1DmacHandlers = 0x002FDBA8; // PARTIAL - once-guarded AddDmacHandler on cause 1 (VIF1 channel, handler 0x2FDCA8) and cause 15 (handler 0x2FDDA8), acks STAT 0x20000, enables DMAC int 1 - cause-15 semantic untraced

InitIopUploadRing = 0x002EFA20; // PARTIAL - binds the CUSTOM game IOP RPC sid 0x11 (client 0x1A7248, retry + sceSifCheckStatRpc), fno 2 fetches IOP buffer base/size into 0x1A7240/0x1A7244, clears the 0x40-entry table 0x215000 - serving IRX + payload identity (likely scene/dialog audio) untraced

// --- text/1EFFC0 handwritten tie/tfrag code - intra-function `j` targets (task #471).
// spimdisasm 1.41.0 turns every `j <target>` into an autogenerated func_ symbol
// (MipsSymbolFunction.py:490) and emits it as `alabel func_` when it lies inside the
// containing function. None of these 67 is jal-ed, pointed to, or preceded by
// `jr $ra` anywhere in SCUS_972.68 (tools/ee/.t471/02_rom_evidence.txt) - they are loop
// labels, so they are pinned as branch labels. The two j-targets reached from a
// DIFFERENT .s (0x2F2E58, 0x2F33E8 <- func_002F35B0) are deliberately left as alabel.

FindWaterWaveGridAtPos = 0x002FDE58; // CONFIRMED mechanism - locates which dynamic water-wave grid contains a world position. Takes x,y,z as floats, scales each by 1024.0f and truncates via FloatToInt, then walks the g_bWaterWavesActive grids (descriptor stride 0x10, payload stride 0x1190 from g_pWaterWaveGrids). Rejects a grid unless the point lies inside its origin +0x0/+0x4/+0x8 and the extents +0xC/+0xE, with a fixed 0x800 slab on z. On a surviving grid it derives cell coords from the grid origin and cell size (D_1AD5A8/D_1AD5AC origin bias, D_1AD5B0/D_1AD5B4 cell size), packs them into a 4x4 occupancy index and tests the bitmask at +0x1E. ApplyHitDamageAndFlash = 0x0030F4F8; // type:func CONFIRMED mechanism - per-frame damage reaction for the owning moby. Consumes a pending hit-event ring record via func_002AA4A8 with 0x30001 and keep zero, deducts the record +0x2C damage from health at state +0x24, starts a hit-flash colour lerp (func_002AC668 start plus func_002AC728 tick, feeding SetMobyColorRGB) with a sound, and on death spawns explosion func_002ACA20 and writes owner state 6. Tail stores the 0xFF no-slot sentinel to moby +0xA8. Only caller is func_0030EE28. Owning weapon UNCONFIRMED, so the name states behaviour only. NOTE Ghidra DROPS an argument on the func_002ACA20 call - the asm stores 0x810001 at 0x18(sp) and -1 at 0x8(sp)
CreepAlongGroundTowardHero = 0x0030F6A0; // type:func CONFIRMED mechanism - gravity-relative ground-hugging pursuit step. Decomposes motion against the world down vector from GetGravityDirectionAtPos 0x2FF768, arms when horizontal distance to hero position 0x189EA0 is under 1.0 and vertical under 1.3, then crawls toward the hero via the generic collide-and-slide mover func_002A90A8, re-seating onto geometry from a downward probe and snapping to g_collHitPoint to avoid tunnelling. Falls at 1 in 150 per frame when the probe misses. Only caller is func_0030EE28. Owning weapon UNCONFIRMED, so the name states behaviour only

// === Track-B thrown-object weapon cluster (2026-07-26, d2). Anchored on inventory item id 0x22 and moby class 0x81F, tied together by callership - all three helpers have 0x315DD0 as their sole caller. NOTE this SUPERSEDES a pre-existing grab-and-throw reading that had propagated through three cross-referencing plate comments - the carried object is SPAWNED by this same code and never grabbed, which is what refutes it. Throwing itself is NOT part of the refutation and stays proven, since ComputeBallisticThrowVelocity is a direct callee. The retail weapon NAME is UNCONFIRMED. ===
UpdateThrownObjectWeapon = 0x00315DD0; // type:func PROBABLE - per-frame update and fire handler for the equipped-weapon moby of inventory item id 0x22. At 0x3164BC it loads a BYTE from D_13958A, which is g_itemEquippedSlot +0x22, and multiplies it by the 0xE0 stride into g_weaponTable, taking the f32 at field +0x54 as a fire RATE and storing 1.0 over it as the shot cooldown at pVars +0x58. ⚠ READ THE ARRAY CAREFULLY. The ROM IMAGE of g_itemEquippedSlot 0x139568 is the IDENTITY sequence 0x00 0x01 0x02 up to 0x4F then zeros, so in the static binary the byte at +0x22 simply IS 0x22 and a static read of it recovers only the offset you indexed with. The array IS genuinely runtime-written though - SetWeaponUpgradeSlot 0x288A58 stores into it at 0x288AE4 (addiu %lo(g_itemEquippedSlot), addu the index, sb) - it holds the active upgrade-variant weaponTable slot per item. Entry 0x22 is nevertheless immovable because its weaponTable next and prev links at 0x23B92A and 0x23B92C are both zero, so [0x22] stays 0x22 for that reason rather than by being a constant. Fire gate is func_0026F738 called with -1 and 1, the same call at the same position in the fire sequence as UpdateLightningBeamWeapon 0x30A6EC which is its only other caller - zero gives a PlayMobySound(4) dry-fire, nonzero gives anim sequence 3 plus state 3 plus an armed cooldown. Dispatch is a 7-entry jump table jtbl_0026E230_text on moby +0x20 bounded by sltiu 7, states 0 to 6 with 4 a hole. Spawns and carries one class 0x81F object then releases it on fire. Zero xrefs so it is registered from a class table outside this tile. NOTE func_0026F738 and its siblings F740 F750 F760 all decompile to return 0 - they are STUBBED in retail, so those branches are effectively dead in this build
ComputeBallisticThrowVelocity = 0x00315C28; // type:func CONFIRMED - ballistic launch velocity toward a target clamped to a maximum range. Speed squared is gravity times d squared over 2 times (d minus h), capped at half gravity times maxRange, then square-rooted. The out vector is sin times speed, cos times speed, and the RAW speed as the vertical term (swc1 f20 at 0x315D94 - an earlier note misread this component). Targets beyond range are clamped to maxRange minus 0.01 via Vec3RescaleToLen. Sole caller is UpdateThrownObjectWeapon at 0x31636C
SpawnThrownWeaponObject = 0x003166A8; // type:func PROBABLE - spawns the class 0x81F object that UpdateThrownObjectWeapon carries and later releases, real signature (Moby *owner, Vec4 *pos) - Ghidra shows a spurious third argument, trust the asm. Called only at the tail of 0x315DD0 and only when the carry slot +0x50 is EMPTY and the +0x54 delay has expired, with its RETURN stored INTO +0x50, so it creates one at a time rather than emitting per frame - that is what refutes the pre-existing sparkle-trail reading. Halves scale, sets state byte 1, stores the owner backref at pVars +0x30, adds a 1 to 3 degree random angular jitter, and stamps pVars +0x5C with g_gameTime as the spawn timestamp. Class 0x81F is spawned at exactly one site in the whole tile, this one
ReleaseCarriedWeaponObject = 0x003167D0; // type:func PROBABLE - releases the carried class 0x81F object with a small random spin, sole caller UpdateThrownObjectWeapon at 0x31659C in the state-3 arm, after which the caller registers the index in the 63-entry dedup list at 0x1F1A78 via func_002AD8B8. Grows scale by 20 over 13, exactly undoing the halving applied at spawn. CollLines from the hero to the release point and on a hit relocates to g_collHitPoint backed off along the normal with velocity scaled 0.6, a de-penetration step. Culls the oldest same-group instance when more than 8 are live by walking g_activeMobyChainHead through +0x28, matching group id +0xAA and tracking the SMALLEST pVars +0x5C, then setting that one's +0x5C to -1 to expire it - exactly the timestamp SpawnThrownWeaponObject stamps
// === Track-B lock-on arc cluster (2026-07-26, d2). FindNearestVisibleTargetMoby = 0x00316A78; // type:func PROBABLE - nearest-visible target scan. Sole caller is UpdateLockOnArcWeapon 0x316C20, which stores the result at aux +0x18 as the lock-on target. NOT the same function as the already-pinned AcquireMobyAutoTarget 0x2B4AC0 - do not conflate the two
UpdateLockOnArcWeapon = 0x00316C20; // type:func PROBABLE - 5-state lock-on then discharge moby update handler and the owner of this whole cluster. DrawLockOnArcFx = 0x00317048; // type:func CONFIRMED mechanism - the registered post-particles draw hook for this cluster, never called directly. Its only reference is the address-take at 0x316C48 and 0x317014 feeding AddFxDrawHookPostParticles from UpdateLockOnArcWeapon, which is a caller pin. Advances the phase D_1AF52C via WrapAnglePiSum into a 0 to 1 oscillator, takes three GS blend configs from func_002846E8, and emits three additive layers of the texture at D_001B2354 +0x14C with sprite counts 0x31, 0x31 and 0x0B. Calls DrawLockOnArcFxSlots for the per-slot geometry
AllocLockOnArcFxSlot = 0x00317200; // type:func CONFIRMED mechanism - claims one free slot of the arc-FX pool at 0x22A990 and randomises its oscillator parameters. Pool stride is 0xA0 and the in-use owner field is +0x8C where 0 means free. The pool has exactly THREE slots - the init loop in UpdateLockOnArcWeapon at 0x316C68 loads the last +0x8C at base +0x1CC with a counter of 2 and walks DOWN by 0xA0 under bgez, storing at +0x1CC then +0x12C then +0x8C and stopping. (An earlier reading of four slots was wrong - it took the +0x1CC start address as a count.) Fills 6 phases at random over -PI to PI, 6 rates at random over 0.001 to 0.005, and 6 amplitudes at random over 3.25 to 4.75 halved, and offsets the endpoint +0x30 by GetMobyCombatState of the target +0x10 when the target is damageable. Called 6 times, all from UpdateLockOnArcWeapon
UpdateLockOnArcFxSlots = 0x003174C8; // type:func PROBABLE - per-frame tick of the same 3-slot arc-FX pool, sole caller UpdateLockOnArcWeapon. Advances six phase angles through WrapAnglePiSum at rate times 100.0 while the life field +0x94 is nonzero and times 50.0 once it is not, derives two local sin and cos offset vectors, and eases the anchor toward the attached moby at +0x90 - a plain copy of the target +0x10 when GetMobyCombatState returns 0, otherwise a lead or predicted position, then Vec3LerpVu0 by 1.0 minus life over maxLife so the anchor migrates from the spawn point to the target as the slot ages. Two countdowns through func_002832F8 - the +0x94 life sets the +0x9C fade flag, and +0x88 frees the slot by zeroing +0x8C
DrawLockOnArcFxSlots = 0x003177D0; // type:func PROBABLE - draw-side walker over the same 3-slot pool, sole caller DrawLockOnArcFx. Matches slots on the same +0x8C owner field the update side uses (read as 0x5C off a base of poolBase +0x30, which is absolute +0x8C - verified against the raw asm, not the decompiler), calls BuildJitteredArcPolyline per matching slot, then gates a three-layer billboard glow on the +0x94 life countdown having reached zero. The glow uses the SAME sprite ids 0x31, 0x31 and 0x0B and the SAME colours 0x3F007F00, 0x5F7F7F7F and 0x10003F00 as its own caller emits at the source position, so the effect is two-ended - a glow at the source and one at each emitter
InitBeamQuadDrawBlock = 0x00317940; // type:func CONFIRMED - initialises an 0x88-byte GS sprite quad draw-descriptor block in place. Frameless leaf with no sp adjust, which is LEGITIMATE here and not filler - it is pure stores to a0 ending in jr $ra with a store in the delay slot. Writes four 16-byte corner records at +0x00, +0x10, +0x20 and +0x30, each being 0.0 then plus or minus 1.0 twice then 1.0, the four corners of a unit quad, and puts the GS tag 0x0000FF9000000260 at +0x80. Sole caller DrawArcRibbonStrip 0x317C08 calls it twice, on sp+0 and sp+0x90, which is consistent with the 0x88-byte block and two layers
BuildJitteredArcPolyline = 0x003179C8; // type:func PROBABLE - generates a 25-point jittered cubic-Hermite arc between one pool slot's two anchors and submits it to DrawArcRibbonStrip. Loop runs 0x18 iterations with t as i over 0x17 through the vec4 Hermite helper func_002A8948. Anchors are slot +0x00 plus slot +0x20 and slot +0x10 plus slot +0x30, that is the two local sin and cos offsets UpdateLockOnArcFxSlots computes added to their base positions. Each sample is displaced by three GetRandomFloatRange over -1.0 to +1.0 passed through Vec3RescaleToLenVu0 to a fixed length of 0.1, the standard electric-arc crackle construction also used by DrawLightningBeamFx 0x30C830. Brightness is picked by age - RandRangeInclusive 0x40 to 0x80 while slot +0x88 is below 7, else 0x00 to 0x40. Sole caller DrawLockOnArcFxSlots
ExtrapolateArcRibbonGuardPoint = 0x00317BA0; // type:func CONFIRMED - linearly extrapolates the trailing guard point of the arc-ribbon polyline. The whole body is three operations - tmp equals p at -0x20 minus p at -0x30 via Vec4SubVu0, then p at -0x10 equals p at -0x20 plus tmp via Vec4AddVu0, with the base computed as arg0 plus arg1 shifted left 4. Offsets read straight from the asm at 0x317BB4, 0x317BBC and 0x317BD8. Sole caller DrawArcRibbonStrip at 0x317D34, which passes the segment count
DrawArcRibbonStrip = 0x00317C08; // type:func CONFIRMED mechanism - renders a point array as two camera-facing UV-scrolling billboard ribbons, the visible arc geometry. Per segment it builds fwd as normalize of p[i+1] minus p[i], right as normalize of the cross of g_cameraPos minus p[i] with fwd, and up as the cross of fwd and right, giving a screen-aligned strip emitted through ProjectAndClipBillboardQuad. Two ribbons at half-widths 0.05 and 0.2 mirrored by a Vec4ScaleVu0 of -1.0, two GetUiTextureTex0 calls with texture 0x2B, and V spans of uOffset or 0x68 and uOffset over 3 or 0x68 so the narrow ribbon scrolls at one third the rate, V advancing 0.33 per segment and wrapping at 1.0. Sole caller BuildJitteredArcPolyline, which passes a count of 0x19 for its 25 points

InterpPlasmaSplinePoint = 0x0031F5D0; // type:func CONFIRMED - cubic interpolation of one vec3 from a sliding 4-point control window, used to smooth the plasma lightning polyline. Frameless leaf with three jr $ra exits. Args (Ghidra reorders them) are f12 t, a0 dst, and a1 through t0 as P0 to P3, confirmed at the call site 0x31FE98. SUPERSEDES a prior Hermite or Catmull-Rom label - there is NO 0.5 factor and NO tangent averaging anywhere in the body - it computes e times t cubed plus d minus e times t squared plus P1 minus P2 times t plus P0, where d is P2 minus P0 and e is P3 minus P1 minus d. Fast paths at t equal 0 and t equal 1 do an lq and sq copy of P0 and P1 and were checked to agree with the general polynomial, so the curve runs P0 to P1 with P2 and P3 only shaping tangents - The t equal 1 test uses bc1fl which is LIKELY so its delay slot is nullified when not taken, and unlike the fast paths the general path stores 0.0 into dst w. // === Track-B agent-deploy gadget cluster (2026-07-26, d2). Every member below is tied to func_003224E0 or func_00321348 by verified callership. 🔴 THE NOUN IS UNSETTLED, AND MY EARLIER FRAMING OF IT WAS WRONG - see the correction that follows, because it changes what a reader should do about it. The label Agent is inherited from the Ghidra plate on func_003224E0, which self-declares its identity UNCONFIRMED and PROBABLE Agents of Doom. I first recorded this as a straight CONFLICT with the existing SlideEnemy pins (InitAndScanSlideEnemyAI 0x003256A0, parent func_00323AB8) on the grounds that both walk classes 0xB0A and 0xB0B and both spawn aux class 0xAB7, and I wrote that AT MOST ONE COULD BE RIGHT. That is REFUTED. (1) The dev string that the SlideEnemy noun was read from is NOT discriminating - Total Slide Time exists in at least two byte-identical copies, 0x1AFF88 and 0x1AFFD0, and is printed from BOTH sides, by func_00323AB8 and by the real entry of the Agent root, so it never distinguished them. (2) The two sides stand in a PRODUCER and CONSUMER relation over the alerted flag data +0x294 - InitAndScanSlideEnemyAI and func_003224E0 both WRITE it into 0xB0A and 0xB0B siblings through a byte-identical group walk, while func_00323AB8 READS it off its OWN data pointer - though it also LATCHES its own copy, writing +0x294 twice at 0x323E30 and 0x324490, so consumer is the right role but not a pure one. (3) The two handler pairs are structural TWINS - same prologue, same sub-root call in the delay slot of the same lw of +0x68, and sixteen shared private-state offsets. The +0x95 mode byte belongs to the two HANDLERS (0x321398 and func_00323AB8), not to the sub-roots - func_003224E0 has no +0x95 access at all. So they are most likely two ROLES in one actor system, or two parallel instances of one handler pattern, and BOTH nouns are probably over-specific rather than one being wrong. Neither owning handler exposes its own class id (both registered from a class table outside this tile, zero xrefs), so nothing here is settled to a shipped name. Every name below states BEHAVIOUR and the noun is a cluster label only. Settling it needs the reader of the data +0x276 flag, which is NOT in this tile. ===

EmitAgentMinionJointImpactBurst = 0x00323488; // type:func PROBABLE - applies three staggered radial damage spheres along the outward axis of attach-point 4 then emits the matching smoke and spark burst. Damage points are P0, P0 plus axis times 0.85 and P0 plus axis times 1.6, each with radius 0.3, through ApplyRadialMobyDamage 0x2A9A90. The smoke loop runs THREE times and the spark loop ONCE - the decrement sits in a jal delay slot and bgez tests the already-decremented counter, so a counter of N gives N+1 iterations, and the measured initial values are 2 at 0x32356C and 0 at 0x32373C. The loop control is a PART-INDEX BITMASK and not a count - an srav of 0x7F8 by the index selects part indices 3 through 10 and skips 0 to 2 and 11 to 14 while the index itself runs 0 to 14. Fragments inherit the PARENT's class read at runtime from lh moby +0xAA, with the part index passed as a3, so no new class id can be harvested here. When data +0x2B4 is nonzero the fragment's update pointer +0x64 is replaced with func_00323A08, a fade then free. Both tables were decoded and every target lands on a real branch target, and +0x20 is state and not an anim id. StepVehicleThrottleAndMove = 0x00327BB0; // type:func CONFIRMED mechanism - integrates forward speed from the stick magnitude and advances the moby. The decay arm multiplies pVars +0x18 by 0.85 when the stick magnitude is zero or the alignment flag pVars +0x34 is clear, otherwise it adds magnitude times 1 over 120 capped at D_1B00D4 times magnitude times 1 over 60, which makes D_1B00D4 the top-speed parameter. moby +0x48 becomes speed times 25.0, plus twice the skid residual pVars +0x38 when unaligned. Motion is Vec3RescaleToLenVu0 of the forward row moby +0xC0 to the speed, then Vec4AddVu0 into the position moby +0x10 - arg order read off the asm at 0x327C98 to 0x327CB0. Tail-calls the 8-point footprint pass func_00327CE0, then SweepVehicleFrontArcForWallCollision, then ConformVehicleToGroundFromProbes. CORRECTED - an earlier version of this pin attributed both the CollLine probing and the state 9 with pVars +0x20 equal to 10 hazard write to the last of those. BOTH of them have their own probe loop AND their own state-9 write - they are different tests, distinguished by CollLine type argument (0 versus 0x12) and ray count (32 versus 8). State 4 for airborne does belong to the ground-conform pass. Also note func_003013D0 is a material CLASSIFIER returning 2 for ids 0, 0xD and 0xE and 1 for 0x1, 0x3, 0xB and 0xC, and the hazard branch fires on NONZERO, so it covers two material classes rather than one. Sole caller is the vehicle handler at 0x327408
BuildVehicleGroundProbePoints = 0x00327CE0; // type:func PROBABLE - builds the 8 world-space ground-probe contact points into 0x22AF20 from the hull matrix rows +0xC0 and +0xD0, each mirrored through a Vec4ScaleVu0 of -1.0 and staged at 0x22AF60. Each point is rescaled to the footprint extent D_1B00BC (gp +0x10CC), offset by the moby position, then pushed back along the up axis 0x1B2730 by D_1B00B8 (gp +0x10C8). EIGHT iterations verified from the initialiser - s1 starts at 0 at 0x327D94, increments at 0x327DD8, exit slti 8 at 0x327E28. ⚠ The backedge at 0x327E2C is a LIKELY bnel that jumps to 0x327DA0 and NOT to the loop head, and its delay-slot slti 4 gates an INNER conditional add covering only the first four points - it is not the loop bound. Sole caller StepVehicleThrottleAndMove
SweepVehicleFrontArcForWallCollision = 0x00327E58; // type:func PROBABLE - a 32-ray 90-degree forward fan sweep that averages the hits and pushes the hull out of walls. THIRTY-TWO iterations verified from the initialiser - s3 starts at 0 at 0x327F6C, exit slti 0x20 at 0x328134 - with the angle being i over 31.0 times PI over 2. Early-outs unless the absolute speed at pVars +0x18 is at least 1e-4, and the SIGN latched at 0x327ECC flips the cosine so the fan mirrors when reversing. Hits are summed then divided by the hit count and added to the moby position. Uses CollLine type argument 0. Sole caller StepVehicleThrottleAndMove
ConformVehicleToGroundFromProbes = 0x003281A0; // type:func PROBABLE - probes the 8 footprint points, averages their normals, slerps the hull orientation onto the result and flags the airborne state. EIGHT iterations verified from the initialiser - s3 is li 7 at 0x3281B4, decremented, and bgez tests the DECREMENTED value, so 7 down to 0. The probe ORDER comes from a 16-byte index table at 0x1B00E8 indexing 0x22AF20 plus index times 0x10, which is the direct producer link back to BuildVehicleGroundProbePoints. The averaged normal is built by pairwise Vec3CrossVu0 with the last iteration wrapping to index 0 to close the polygon - the quaternion half-angle uses D_1B00E4 and the position is eased with Vec3LerpVu0 at D_1B00E0. State 4 on nothing hit, at 0x32861C, is the airborne flag. Uses CollLine type argument 0x12. Sole caller StepVehicleThrottleAndMove
SteerVehicleTowardCameraRelativeStick = 0x00328668; // type:func PROBABLE - turns the hull toward the camera-relative stick direction at a smoothed rate. Takes the 2D stick at pVars +0x10 and +0x14 against the camera matrix at 0x1B54F0 (confirmed a camera matrix by xrefs - written by UpdateCamera and ApplyCameraTransition, read by BuildFrameViewMatrices), projects out the up-axis component, and turns by (PI over 2 minus asin of the dot) times 0.1, so a tenth of the remaining error per frame. pVars +0x38 receives 1.0 minus the dot and is damped by 0.7 UNCONDITIONALLY, before the zero-length early-out - pVars +0x34 is set to 1 when the dot exceeds 0.75. A degenerate zero-length cross at 0x3287AC falls back to matrix row 0. Sole caller is the vehicle handler at real entry 0x00326FE8

Directions come from the 96-entry two-ring unit-vector table D_26A060 (read from the image - x is 0.4755 for entries 0 to 47 and 0.5 for 48 to 95) and the quad index table D_26A660 whose 0x10 stride holds four direction-and-phase halfword pairs forming a ring-to-ring quad strip. // === MIS-SPLIT BOUNDARY DEFECTS found while naming this tile (2026-07-26, d2). These FOURTEEN are NOT pinned - a name would land on the wrong bytes. Same class as the w_sqrt head-fusion recorded near the top of this file, and they need the splat re-split, not a symbol_addrs pin. ===
// func_00329010 (splat size 0x3D0) is HEAD-FUSED - its first four words are addiu $sp,$sp,+0x40 then nop then addiu $sp,$sp,+0x140 then nop, two POSITIVE stack adjustments with no jr $ra, and they are the stranded epilogue tails of func_00328EE8 and func_00328B70 respectively. The REAL prologue addiu $sp,$sp,-0x60 is at 0x00329020
// func_00326F90 (splat size 0x878) is HEAD-FUSED by 0x58 bytes - the range 0x326F90 to 0x326FE4 is orphan epilogue filler, positive "addiu $sp,$sp,+N" teardowns (0x10, 0x30, 0xC0, 0x30, 0x60, 0x20, 0x50, 0x140, 0x160) interleaved with nops and NO jr $ra, and the REAL function runs from 0x00326FE8 (prologue addiu $sp,$sp,-0xF0) to its jr $ra at 0x327800. StepProjectileCollideAndSlide = 0x002FF860; // CONFIRMED mechanism - one collision-resolution step for a deployed-weapon projectile state block. Queries the gravity direction via func_002FF768, optionally offsets the probe along the moby up-axis +0xE0 scaled by +0x5C, then CollSphere with radius +0x3C and a CollLine fallback (result 1 vs 2 selects g_collHitPointNudged vs g_collHitPoint). Records the hit material into +0x54, rejects hits whose object id appears in the ignore list at +0x34 count +0x38 (returns 0), then compares the incidence dot against the threshold +0x44 - shallow hits project the velocity onto the hit plane and rescale by restitution +0x40 (returns 1), steep hits just snap the position (returns 2). // === Track-B combat/damage core (the shared damage pipeline every weapon funnels through; Track-B 2026-06-16). ===
// The firing -> projectile -> damage path: a weapon updater builds a 0x28-byte DAMAGE PACKET, then
// either posts it to one moby or broadcasts it over a sphere. Consumers are overlay per-class handlers
// that read g_collHitEventRing and subtract from the target's combat HP (moby->pVars[0] -> +0x00 float).

Damage source = g_weaponTable[g_itemEquippedSlot[0]]

// TypeB/TypeC variant vtables are referenced in code as D_1ADA38 / D_1AD9F8
// (installed at elem +0x30 by GuiElementInitTypeB/C). Descriptive g_ rename is a
// future Track-B cleanup (would touch the existing D_1ADA38/D_1AD9F8 #else refs).
//
// === Recovered GUI struct types -> going-decompiled/include/gui.h (Track-B) ===
// GuiElement  - leaf drawable node, base sizeof 0x4C / text variant 0x58. vtable at
//   +0x30 (slot +0xC = draw, +0x14 = dtor). Pooled vec4 ptrs: +0x00 pos, +0x04 scale,
//   +0x08 aux, +0x0C color, +0x10 visible. +0x28 name, +0x2C pool. Subtype union at
//   +0x34..+0x54 (sprite uv/alpha/tex, text string/align/autofit, list cap/count/sentinel).
//   Field-map evidence: the GuiElement* accessors/ctors above (all CONFIRMED).
// GuiWidget   - container/screen that OWNS elements. Recovered in full as the GuiMenuList
//   variant (sizeof 0xD0): embeds a scratch GuiElement at +0x00, then +0x5C originVec,
//   +0x60 selectedRow, +0x68 rowArray (stride 0x14), +0x6C rowEnabled[16], +0xAC/+0xB0/+0xB4
//   color triple, +0xB8/+0xBC row Y base/step, +0xC0 rowCount, +0xC4 useRawText, +0xC8/+0xCC
//   underline. Evidence: the GuiMenuList* accessors + GuiMenuListDraw above.
// NOTE: Gui*ScreenInit screen objects do NOT share one struct - each ctor lays its children
//   out inline at fixed offsets; gui.h documents the common screen-header prefix + the
//   0x88-stride child-widget activation record (child+0x18 alpha/+0x1C state/+0x28 init).
//   The GuiManager singleton g_guiInstance (addr 0x1A8D04, sizeof 0x3FB20) is the root, NOT a GuiWidget.
// Layout is region-agnostic: EU v1.00 twins share it (gui.h serves both builds).

// === Track-B call-graph cluster around the two water-contact anchors (lvl overlay). 2026-06-21. UpdateSinkingWaterObject = 0x0032A588; // type:func CONFIRMED - moby update tick driving an object sinking into water - sole per-frame caller of UpdateWaterMobyContact (0x32AD60) and the only caller of the two helpers below. Re-registers a post-particle draw hook every frame via AddFxDrawHookPostParticles, passing draw-callback constant 0x32af58 (a USA code pointer, NOT this function address - do not confuse with the EU parent twin which coincidentally lands at 0x32AF58 via the +0x9D0 shift). // === Track-B DATA lane (2026-07-25) - 989snd EE-side RPC channel globals + frame counter + global state bitfield. Each traced from its WRITE site. ===
g_frameCounter = 0x001B1518; // u32 frame counter bumped once per vblank-waited frame outside the main loop - idiom lw / addiu +1 / sw at every write site, always adjacent to jal WaitVblankGetField 0x1261F0 (CaptureScreenToVram 0x2CAE10, RestoreScreenFromVram, MenuScreenLoad, RunLevelTransition, PlayBonusMovie, SceneTransitionTeardownA). g_gameStateFlags = 0x001A7424; // u32 global state bitfield, gp-relative -0x7bcc(gp) - MEASURED, the lui/lw 0x7424 reads and the -0x7bcc(gp) writes are the same storage. ~90 xrefs, every write is a read-modify-write with and/ori of single-bit masks (0x1 0x2 0x4 0x8 0x10 0x20 0x40 0x200 0x400). LIKELY for the word as a whole - GAP, the meaning of each individual BIT is NOT traced, do not attribute a feature to a bit without re-deriving it

// === Track-B d1 (2026-07-25) - SDK libc printf family + IPU/MPEG bitstream reader (cod/015180). ===
// Population measured before working - 1614 of 2817 USA Ghidra functions are FUN_-form, of which 151 are already named in this file, leaving 1463 truly unknown (290 of them below 0x131B48).
sprintf = 0x00115DA8; // CONFIRMED - sprintf(buf, fmt, ...). Builds a stack FILE-like block (cursor +0x00, base +0x10, length sentinel 0x7FFFFFFF at +0x08 and +0x14 so it is plain sprintf NOT snprintf, flags 0x208 at +0x0C, _impure_ptr 0x133E74 at +0x54) then calls VfprintfDispatch 0x118CC8 and NUL-terminates at the advanced cursor. Flags bit 0x200 is exactly the bit the engines test to memcpy into the buffer instead of writing to a stream. Spills a2-t3 and f12-f18 as varargs. 43 callers, all UI/text builders. Corroborates the pre-existing FormatIntWithThousandsSep note which already called this the sprintf-like func
VfprintfDispatch = 0x00118CC8; // CONFIRMED - selects which printf engine to run. Pre-scans fmt and tail-calls VfprintfFloat 0x118D98 if a float conversion is present, else VfprintfInteger 0x119BF8. The switch is ((c - 0x45) << 24) >> 24 with cases 0, 2, 7, 0x20, 0x21, 0x22 which decode arithmetically to E, G, L, e, f, g - the float and long-double set. Standard newlib split so the integer-only engine avoids linking the float formatter
VfprintfInteger = 0x00119BF8; // CONFIRMED - newlib vfprintf engine, integer-only variant. Hard anchor is the literal internal assertion string bug in vfprintf - bad base. Handles flags, width and precision including star form, h and l and ll length modifiers, and conversions d i o u x X c s p n via repeated div and mod by base (helpers 0x121B20 and 0x1220F0). Has NO E G e f g case at all - that omission is the discriminator against VfprintfFloat. Output goes to the buffer via memcpy 0x1153D4 when flags bit 0x200 is set, else to the stream writer 0x119AC0. Returns the character count
VfprintfFloat = 0x00118D98; // CONFIRMED - newlib vfprintf engine, float-capable variant. Structurally identical to VfprintfInteger 0x119BF8 including the same bad base assertion and the same 0x200 buffer-vs-stream split, differing ONLY by additionally having cases 0x45 0x47 0x65 0x66 0x67 for E G e f g plus L as a no-op modifier. That presence versus absence is the whole naming discriminator. For g and G it derives the decimal exponent (0x1182B8, compares 0x123028, pow10 0x123078) to pick e-style or f-style then formats via 0x1185E8. Sole caller is VfprintfDispatch - MEASURED
IpuGetBits = 0x0012C878; // CONFIRMED - reads n bits from the IPU bitstream and ADVANCES. Hardware anchor read from raw disassembly - 0x10002010 is IPU_CTRL and 0x10002000 is IPU_CMD. Spins while (CTRL and 0x80004000) equals 0x80000000, pumping ctx+0x858 via 0x12FAE8 every 5000 iterations, refills with bare cmd 0x40000000 when ctx+0x83C holds fewer than n bits, issues cmd (n or 0x40000000), then returns the previously cached word shifted right by (32 - n). Consume-not-peek is PROVEN by IpuParseGopHeader which reads 1,5,6,1,6,6 sequentially as MPEG GOP time-code fields. ctx +0x818 last cmd word, +0x838 cached bits, +0x83C valid bit count. 16 callers, all in the MPEG decoder cluster
IpuSkipBits = 0x0012C788; // LIKELY - advances the IPU bitstream by n bits and discards them. Same body as IpuGetBits minus the return path - waits on IPU_CTRL, issues cmd (n or 0x40000000), refreshes ctx+0x818 and ctx+0x838 via IpuWaitCmdResult, sets ctx+0x83C to 0x20, returns void. GAP - the discard reading rests on the absent return value plus call sites (0x12C9C8 loops it with 8 to step whole bytes hunting a start code, 0x12CA48 calls it with 7), but I did not trace IPU FIFO state across a call to prove there is no further side effect, and unlike IpuGetBits it re-primes unconditionally. Pure-skip versus skip-with-refill is UNPROVEN
IpuWaitReady = 0x0012C3B0; // CONFIRMED - blocks until the IPU can accept a new command. Pure wait, issues no command and returns nothing. Spins while (IPU_CTRL 0x10002010 and 0x80004000) equals 0x80000000, that is while BUSY is set and FIFO-ready bit 0x4000 is clear, calling the pump helper 0x12FAE8 on ctx+0x858 every 5000 iterations. Leaf of the IPU bit-reader family - IpuGetBits and IpuSkipBits inline this same spin
IpuWaitCmdResult = 0x0012C458; // CONFIRMED - waits for the pending IPU command to retire and returns its result word. Reads IPU_CMD 0x10002000 - bit 31 set means still executing, so it spins on IPU_CTRL waiting for bit 0x4000 and returns as soon as IPU_CMD goes non-negative. Pumps ctx+0x858 via 0x12FAE8 every 5000 spins. Callers store the returned word into ctx+0x838, which is how IpuGetBits and IpuSkipBits obtain refilled bits
IpuParseVideoStartCodes = 0x0012CAB0; // CONFIRMED - MPEG video start-code dispatch loop. Byte-aligns via 0x12C9C8, reads 32 bits with IpuGetBits and branches on the standard MPEG-1 and MPEG-2 start codes - 0x1B3 sequence header goes to 0x130300, 0x1B8 group-of-pictures goes to IpuParseGopHeader, 0x100 picture goes to 0x12CBC0 then posts a type-5 request to ctx+0x858 via 0x12FA98, sets ctx+0x828 and ctx+0x830 to all-ones and returns ctx+0x150, and 0x1B7 sequence end returns 0. This start-code set is what identifies the 0x12Cxxx and 0x12Dxxx cluster as the MPEG FMV decoder
IpuParseGopHeader = 0x0012D060; // CONFIRMED - parses an MPEG group_of_pictures header, reached from IpuParseVideoStartCodes on start code 0x1B8. Consumes bits via IpuGetBits in exactly the MPEG GOP field widths in order - 1 drop_frame_flag, 5 hours, 6 minutes, 1 marker, 6 seconds, 6 pictures, then 1 closed_gop stored at ctx+0x1A4 and 1 broken_link stored at ctx+0x1A8. That 1,5,6,1,6,6 sequence is both the identity discriminator and the positive proof that IpuGetBits advances. Presets ctx+0xE8 to 0, ctx+0x854 to 1, ctx+0x84C to ctx+0x850 plus 1, then continues into the picture layer at 0x12CC88

ScatterTrailToRandomAnchorPoint = 0x002EF610; // CONFIRMED mechanism - places a trail state at a random point around its anchor record and restarts its lifetime. Draws two independent random angles and radii (GetRandomAngle then the VU0 trig leaves 0x283B30 and 0x283B48, each scaled by GetRandomFloatRange against the record radius) to build an offset, adds it to the anchor centre at record +0x30, applies a -64.0 to 64.0 jitter to the vertical component relative to the owner +0x18, then calls ClampTrailToAnchorAndCollide and seeds the countdown at state+0x130 to 300
SetTrailPositionAndCollide = 0x002EF778; // CONFIRMED - copies the caller vec4 at a1 into the trail state position state+0x60, then re-resolves it via ClampTrailToAnchorAndCollide unless the phase is 2 or 0xC. No-ops when the moby has no trail state. 5 call sites, all in the tile driver func_002EE868
RetreatTrailToAnchorAndCollide = 0x002EF708; // CONFIRMED mechanism - pulls a trail state back toward its anchor and restarts its lifetime. Rescales the vector at state+0xC0 to length 8.0, adds it to the owner position +0x10, stores the result as the new position at state+0x60, resolves it via ClampTrailToAnchorAndCollide and seeds the countdown at state+0x130 to 300
EndTrailIfAnchorBlocked = 0x002EF538; // CONFIRMED mechanism - ends a trail when its owner has entered any of the blocking volumes listed in the trail global block. Runs only when the count at g_trailAnchorSlot+0x90 (0x214FD0 - the GLOBAL block, not the selected anchor record) is non-zero, forces phase 0xD to phase 1 first, then tests the owner position +0x10 against each of the count entries in the pointer list at g_trailAnchorSlot+0x9C (0x214FDC) via func_002ADB10 - on the first hit it calls EndMobyTrail, sets the phase to 0xD and calls RetreatTrailToAnchorAndCollide. Returns nothing
IsTrailWithinAnchorRange = 0x002EF958; // CONFIRMED - range and lifetime test for one trail state, returning 1 when the trail should stop and 0 otherwise. Compares Vec3DistVu0 between the owner position +0x10 and the state position +0x60 against a threshold of 1.0, raised to 8.0 when the phase is 2 - when the distance is under the threshold it reports true only for phases other than 8 (an xori 8 then sltu). Independently of the distance it also ticks the countdown at state+0x130 via TickCountdownTimer and forces the result true when that timer reports a non-zero code, so an expired trail always stops. 6 call sites
SelectTrailCameraTarget = 0x002EDEB0; // LIKELY - frameless leaf that selects a trail camera target slot. Reads the state word at g_cameraSlotActive+0x9C0 offset +0x30 and picks slot 1 when it equals 9 (an xori 9 then movz), otherwise leaves the slot at -1 and returns without storing. On a valid slot it publishes the pointer D_262840 into +0x50 and copies the per-slot word from +0x18C into D_262840 offset +0x68. Control flow and the operands are exact - the meaning of the state value 9 and of D_262840 is not traced
RebuildTfragLodTables = 0x002EFED8; // CONFIRMED mechanism - recomputes the tfrag LOD distance table and the LOD cross-fade coefficients from the three source distances at g_tfragCount+0xC/+0x10/+0x14. Writes g_tfragLodDists[0..2] as each source scaled by 1024.0 and rounded through FloatToInt, then zeroes 0x40 bytes at g_trailLodBlendCoeffs via the handwritten qword filler 0x283438 and stores per-band slope and offset pairs derived from 1.0/(d0-d1) and 1.0/(d1-d2), all pre-multiplied by the scale float at 0x1B91E0. Called as the final step of BuildCameraProjection, so the tables are rebuilt whenever the projection changes. The consumer of the coefficient block is CullAndEmitTfrags, which is already CONFIRMED to select tfrag LOD by g_tfragLodDists. EU UNMEASURED
ColorLerpPacked = 0x002846E8; // CONFIRMED - blends two packed 8-bit-per-channel colour words. Unpacks a0 and a1 from bytes to four 32-bit lanes with pextlb then pextlh, converts both to float with vitof0, evaluates a0*(1-t) + a1*t in VU0 where t is the float in f12 (the vsubx.w against vf0 forms 1-t), then converts back with vftoi0 and repacks with ppach then ppacb, returning the packed word in v0. a0 is the t=0 endpoint and a1 the t=1 endpoint, read directly off the vmulaw/vmaddx pair. Frameless handwritten leaf, 68 call sites across 18 files - the widest-reach symbol in this batch. EU UNMEASURED
Vec2LengthXyVu0 = 0x002837D0; // CONFIRMED - 2-component length of the vector at a0, using only the x and y lanes. Squares the quadword with vmul.xyz, folds y into x with vaddy.x, takes vsqrt of the x lane and returns the result in f0. Structurally identical to the already-CONFIRMED Vec3LengthVu0 at 0x2837A0 except that the z lane is never added in, which is the discriminator. Frameless leaf, 32 call sites. EU UNMEASURED
AngleAbsDiffPi = 0x00284630; // CONFIRMED - unsigned angular distance between two angles in radians, folded into 0 to pi. Computes abs(a-b) and, when that is not less than pi, returns 2*pi - it instead, so the result is always the shorter way around. Uses the 0x40490FDB encoding of pi. Frameless leaf, 19 call sites. Complements the already-CONFIRMED signed WrapAnglePiDiff at 0x284590, which keeps the sign and range -pi to pi. EU UNMEASURED
QuatToMatrix3 = 0x00284308; // CONFIRMED - converts the quaternion at a0 into a 3-row rotation matrix written to a1. Note the argument order is source then destination. Loads the quat once, forms the doubled products via vadd then the vmulw/vmulx/vmuly/vmulz family, and stores exactly three rows with sqc2 at a1+0x0, a1+0x10 and a1+0x20 - the three stores are the proof it emits a 3x3 basis in quadwords, not a full 4x4. Frameless leaf, 25 call sites. EU UNMEASURED
OrientTrailCtxToVelocity = 0x002EEE58; // CONFIRMED mechanism - builds the trail context orientation from its velocity vector. No-ops when the moby has no trail context, and skips straight to the camera-aligned branch when the phase is 8 or 9. Otherwise derives pitch from Atan2fPoly of the xy-plane length (via Vec2LengthXyVu0 of ctx+0x90) against the z component ctx+0x98, and yaw from Atan2fPoly of ctx+0x90 against ctx+0x94, storing them at ctx+0x34 and ctx+0x38. Banks the roll at ctx+0x30 by the turn delta, clamped symmetrically to plus or minus 1.4137 radians (exactly 81 degrees), except in phases 2 and 0xC which skip the bank entirely. Converts the resulting euler triple to a quaternion and writes the matrix to ctx+0xC0 via QuatToMatrix3. In the phase 8 or 9 branch it instead aligns the three basis rows at ctx+0xC0/0xD0/0xE0 to g_activeCamera. EU UNMEASURED
SteerTrailCtxTowardOwner = 0x002EF090; // CONFIRMED mechanism - integrates one trail context velocity toward its owner moby with a bounded turn rate. No-ops without a trail context. Refreshes the per-kind tuning triple by calling 0x2EF208 first, zeroes the scratch at ctx+0xC0, then forms the offset from the context position ctx+0x60 to the owner position at moby+0x10, rescales it to length g_trailAccelMin and adds it into the velocity. Derives a steering magnitude from (ctx+0x124 - g_trailSpeedMax*0.5)/g_trailSpeedMax, takes its absolute value, clamps it low against g_trailAccelMin then high against g_trailAccelMax, negates it when the unclamped value was positive, and adds that as a second acceleration term. Finally clamps the velocity length to g_trailAccelMin*g_trailSpeedMax and accumulates it into the position accumulator at ctx+0x90. EU UNMEASURED
AgeTrailParticlesForOwner = 0x002EE1C0; // CONFIRMED mechanism - ages and retires every trail particle owned by the moby in a0. Returns immediately when the pool base g_pTrailPool is null. Walks all 512 records of stride 0x50 and skips any whose owner key +0x48 does not equal a0. For a live record it computes the normalized lifetime as IntToFloat(+0x40)/IntToFloat(+0x44), scales the base size +0x34 by it into +0x38, fades the colour by blending +0x2C toward 0x3F3F3F through ColorLerpPacked with the cube of that ratio, advances the spin angle +0x24 by +0x20 through WrapAnglePiSum, and when the flag +0x3C is set jitters the value at +0x28 by a random 0.001 to 0.003. Then integrates position +0x10 by velocity and damps the velocity by 0.99. Frees the record by zeroing +0x48 when the countdown at +0x40 reports expiry through TickCountdownTimer. EU UNMEASURED

g_trailAccelMin = 0x001AC2C0; // CONFIRMED as the LOW clamp of the trail acceleration pair. Written only together with g_trailAccelMax by the per-kind tuning selector 0x2EF208, always as the first of a triple, with values in the 0.005 to 0.04 range that read as per-frame rates. SteerTrailCtxTowardOwner applies it as the floor in a low-then-high clamp, which is what fixes it as the minimum rather than the maximum. Also used directly as the rescale length for the owner-directed acceleration. EU UNMEASURED
g_trailAccelMax = 0x001AC2C4; // CONFIRMED as the HIGH clamp of the trail acceleration pair. Written only together with g_trailAccelMin by the per-kind tuning selector 0x2EF208, values 0.025 to 0.2. SteerTrailCtxTowardOwner applies it as the ceiling immediately after the g_trailAccelMin floor. Both members of the pair are scaled by 0.7 together for the kinds outside 2, 0xB and 0xC. EU UNMEASURED
g_trailSpeedMax = 0x001AC2C8; // CONFIRMED - per-kind speed cap for a trail context. Written as the first member of the tuning triple by 0x2EF208 with per-kind values from 0.133 to 0.5, and read by the already-pinned velocity clamp at 0x2EF468, which rescales the velocity to this length whenever it exceeds it and records the clamp at ctx+0x124. SteerTrailCtxTowardOwner also normalizes its steering magnitude by this value. EU UNMEASURED

When the part array at D_2148D0 is empty it first calls BuildShipPartMobys 0x2E6FB0 to spawn it, seeds a spring-follow state via InitMobySpringFollowState against 0x214920, and in game state 0 samples ProbeGroundHeight under the owner to hold a hover height in D_1AC008. Closes with StepMobySpringFollow. Called on the moby whose class +0xAA is 0xD54 - the ship - by the cinematic driver TickCinematicPlayback and by the level-exit scene. 4 call sites. EU UNMEASURED
DrawShipGroundShadowQuad = 0x002E79D0; // CONFIRMED mechanism - draws the ground shadow quad under the ship moby. Registered as a pre-particle FX hook via AddFxDrawHookPreParticles at 0x2EA9E8, which is why no jal reaches it. DrawShipReflectionSurface = 0x002ED9C8; // CONFIRMED mechanism - draws the ship's reflective surface as a 0x26-quad billboard mesh. Registered as a post-particle FX hook via AddFxDrawHookPostParticles at 0x2EA9DC, and also called directly by DrawShipCustomizeMenu 0x2D54DC, which is the independent confirmation that the surface belongs to the ship. Per vertex it transforms the model normal and position through the two 0x2B-entry source tables D_262080 and D_262590, reflects the eye vector about the normal via Vec3DotVu0 then a two-times-dot subtract, and maps the reflected direction to a UV pair around 0.5 scaled by 2*sqrt(z+1) - the standard sphere-map reflection parameterization, which is the identity discriminator. Cross-fades the new UVs against the previous frame set D_214DE8 over a 0x3C-frame timer ticked by TickCountdownTimer, then emits 0x26 quads through the index table D_262330 via ProjectAndClipBillboardQuad. TickCinematicPlayback = 0x002EC038; // CONFIRMED mechanism - advances the in-engine cinematic by one frame and returns 1 when it should stop. No-ops once the finished flag D_1B8818 is set, and ends playback by setting that flag and clearing g_nGameStatePending when the frame counter reaches the total at scene+0x40. Each frame it calls ResetFxDrawQueues then PlaceCinematicShipCameraPath, fades g_screenFadeBlack down by 0.34 to a floor of 0, advances both the sub-chunk frame at scene+0x38 and the absolute frame, and calls StopDialogVoice once the frame passes the audio length at scene+0x48. Past frame 0x11 with the fade complete it samples the pad for a skip. When the sub-chunk frame reaches 0x60 on NTSC or 0x50 on PAL it bumps the chunk index, calls LoadCinematicChunkCast and prefetches the next chunk via StreamDialogVoiceChunk. It then interpolates every cast moby between two animation keyframes by the sub-chunk parity, and for the cast member of class 0xD54 calls PoseShipPartMobys and registers DrawShipReflectionSurface as a post-particle hook. Cast class 0 is published as g_pHeroMoby. Sole caller PlayCinematic. EU UNMEASURED
LoadCinematicChunkCast = 0x002EC3F0; // CONFIRMED mechanism - decompresses one cinematic chunk and binds its cast mobys. Runs DecompressWad from the source at scene+0x6C into the buffer at scene+0x70 with a FlushCache on both sides, then parses the chunk header into the scene block at g_cameraSlotActive+0x990 - total frames to +0x40, audio length to +0x48, cast count to +0x44, camera keys to +0x4C, and zeroes the sub-chunk frame at +0x38. The camera-key pointer is taken only when the header offset is at least 0x400, else the field is nulled. For each cast entry it remaps class 0x215 to 0xD54, then spawns the moby with SpawnMoby - or InitMobyFromClass into g_pReservedMoby0 when the class is 0 - stamps the shared render state 0x1FF at +0x32, flag bits 6 at +0x34, 0xFF at +0x62 and colour 0x202020, allocates a per-cast animation slot by post-incrementing the class refcount at +0xC, stores the pointer into the cast array at scene+0x18C and publishes the 0xD54 member to scene+0x54. Finally it relocates the animation frame pointer table in place by biasing each entry by the chunk base. Called by PlayCinematic and per-chunk by TickCinematicPlayback. EU UNMEASURED
PlaceCinematicShipCameraPath = 0x002EDEF8; // CONFIRMED mechanism - positions the four cinematic camera path nodes relative to the ship cast member. Runs only on scene index 0 and only for frames 0x32 through 0x270F, so it is a per-cinematic scripted segment.
